{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011Cfw8k8cykrBF5FKWfW2Bz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CGpY3auzgDUrMnVZqDjDf6","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > black-scholes/black_scholes.cpp <<'EOF'\n// Black-Scholes option pricing benchmark - hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA version.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy\n//   * MPI     : the option index range [0, N) is block-partitioned across ranks\n//               (one or more ranks per node / per GPU). Rank 0 gathers the\n//               results for printing / validation.\n//   * CUDA    : every option owned by a rank is priced on a GPU. Options are\n//               generated directly on the device from their global index (the\n//               same arithmetic as generateOptions()), so no host->device\n//               traffic is needed; only the 8-byte price travels back.\n//   * OpenMP  : when a rank owns several GPUs (fewer ranks than GPUs on a\n//               node) one OpenMP thread drives each GPU concurrently. Each\n//               thread double-buffers its work through two CUDA streams so\n//               kernel execution overlaps the device->host copies. OpenMP is\n//               also used for host-side data generation.\n//\n// Results are bit-for-bit independent of the number of ranks / GPUs since the\n// same kernel evaluates the same index with the same arithmetic everywhere.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <array>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <climits>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#ifndef M_PI\n#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT_2\n#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT1_2\n#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI\n#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948\n#endif\n\nenum OptionType {\n    CALL = 0,\n    PUT = 1\n};\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Error handling helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                             \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                             \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,    \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__));                       \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                       \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n#define MPI_CHECK(call)                                                         \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        int err__ = (call);                                                     \\\n        if (err__ != MPI_SUCCESS) {                                             \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"MPI error %s at %s:%d\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__); \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                       \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pricing math (shared by host and device)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ inline double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double sqrtT = sqrt(T);\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrtT);\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrtT;\n\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    const double divDiscount = exp(-q * T);\n\n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * divDiscount * cumulativeNormal(d1) - K * discount * cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * divDiscount * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\nconstexpr int kNumTestOptions = 7;\n\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, kNumTestOptions> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\n// Device-side copy of the test options (filled once per device before pricing)\n__constant__ OptionInput c_testOptions[kNumTestOptions];\n\n// Generate option i by cycling through the test options and scaling spot/strike.\n// Identical arithmetic on host and device.\n__host__ __device__ inline OptionInput makeOption(const OptionInput* base,\n                                                  const size_t i) noexcept {\n    OptionInput opt = base[i % kNumTestOptions];\n    const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(kNumTestOptions));\n    opt.spot *= factor;\n    opt.strike *= factor;\n    return opt;\n}\n\n// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set (host, OpenMP)\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {\n    static constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        options[i] = makeOption(testOptions.data(), i);\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernel: generate + price options [first, first + count) into out[]\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256)\npriceOptionsKernel(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t first, const size_t count) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;\n    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n         k < count; k += stride) {\n        const OptionInput opt = makeOption(c_testOptions, first + k);\n        out[k] = blackScholes(opt);\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-device worker context\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nconstexpr size_t kChunkOptions = size_t(1) << 22;   // 4M options = 32 MiB per buffer\nconstexpr int kStreamsPerDevice = 2;\n\nstruct DeviceWorker {\n    int device = -1;\n    size_t globalFirst = 0;   // first global option index handled by this device\n    size_t count = 0;         // number of options handled by this device\n    double* hostOut = nullptr; // where results land on the host (hostOut[0..count))\n    double* dBuf[kStreamsPerDevice] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    cudaStream_t stream[kStreamsPerDevice] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    int blocksPerGrid = 0;\n\n    void init() {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(0)); // force context creation now, outside the timed region\n\n        static constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(c_testOptions, testOptions.data(),\n                                      sizeof(OptionInput) * kNumTestOptions));\n\n        const size_t bufElems = std::min(kChunkOptions, std::max<size_t>(count, 1));\n        for (int s = 0; s < kStreamsPerDevice; ++s) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream[s], cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dBuf[s], bufElems * sizeof(double)));\n        }\n\n        int numSMs = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&numSMs, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, device));\n        blocksPerGrid = numSMs * 8;\n\n        // Warm up: load the kernel module (CUDA lazy loading) outside the timed region.\n        priceOptionsKernel<<<1, 256, 0, stream[0]>>>(dBuf[0], 0, 0);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream[0]));\n    }\n\n    // Price all options of this device; blocks until results are in hostOut.\n    void run() {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n        size_t done = 0;\n        int s = 0;\n        while (done < count) {\n            const size_t n = std::min(kChunkOptions, count - done);\n            const int blocks = static_cast<int>(\n                std::min<size_t>(blocksPerGrid, (n + 255) / 256));\n            // The stream is in-order: the kernel for the next use of this\n            // buffer waits for the previous copy out of it to complete.\n            priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, 256, 0, stream[s]>>>(dBuf[s], globalFirst + done, n);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostOut + done, dBuf[s], n * sizeof(double),\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream[s]));\n            done += n;\n            s = (s + 1) % kStreamsPerDevice;\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < kStreamsPerDevice; ++i) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream[i]));\n        }\n    }\n\n    void destroy() {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n        for (int s = 0; s < kStreamsPerDevice; ++s) {\n            if (dBuf[s]) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dBuf[s]));\n            if (stream[s]) CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream[s]));\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Validation / CLI\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Block partition of [0, total) across `parts`; returns [first, first+count) of part p.\nstatic inline void blockRange(const size_t total, const int parts, const int p,\n                              size_t& first, size_t& count) {\n    const size_t base = total / parts;\n    const size_t rem = total % parts;\n    const size_t up = static_cast<size_t>(p);\n    first = up * base + std::min(up, rem);\n    count = base + (up < rem ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n// Gather per-rank contiguous result blocks onto rank 0 (handles > INT_MAX elements).\nstatic void gatherResults(std::vector<double>& results, const double* local,\n                          const size_t localCount, const size_t numOptions,\n                          const int rank, const int size) {\n    constexpr size_t kMaxMsg = size_t(1) << 28; // elements per message (2 GiB)\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        std::vector<MPI_Request> reqs;\n        for (int r = 1; r < size; ++r) {\n            size_t first, count;\n            blockRange(numOptions, size, r, first, count);\n            for (size_t off = 0; off < count; off += kMaxMsg) {\n                const size_t n = std::min(kMaxMsg, count - off);\n                reqs.emplace_back();\n                MPI_CHECK(MPI_Irecv(results.data() + first + off, static_cast<int>(n),\n                                    MPI_DOUBLE, r, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &reqs.back()));\n            }\n        }\n        if (!reqs.empty()) {\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(reqs.size()), reqs.data(), MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE));\n        }\n    } else {\n        for (size_t off = 0; off < localCount; off += kMaxMsg) {\n            const size_t n = std::min(kMaxMsg, localCount - off);\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(local + off, static_cast<int>(n), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided));\n    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size));\n\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ---- GPU assignment: ranks sharing a node split that node's GPUs -------\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank,\n                                  MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm));\n    int nodeRank = 0, nodeSize = 1;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &nodeSize));\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    if (cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount) != cudaSuccess) deviceCount = 0;\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Rank %d: no CUDA device available\\n\", rank);\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    // Devices owned by this rank: device d belongs to node-local rank d % nodeSize.\n    // If there are more ranks than GPUs, ranks share GPUs round-robin.\n    std::vector<int> myDevices;\n    if (nodeSize >= deviceCount) {\n        myDevices.push_back(nodeRank % deviceCount);\n    } else {\n        for (int d = nodeRank; d < deviceCount; d += nodeSize) myDevices.push_back(d);\n    }\n    const int numMyDevices = static_cast<int>(myDevices.size());\n\n    int totalDevices = 0;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&numMyDevices, &totalDevices, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d, GPUs in use: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\\n\",\n               size, totalDevices, omp_get_max_threads());\n    }\n\n    // ---- Work decomposition -------------------------------------------------\n    size_t localFirst = 0, localCount = 0;\n    blockRange(numOptions, size, rank, localFirst, localCount);\n\n    // Generate options: rank 0 needs the leading options on the host for\n    // validation; everything else is generated directly on the GPUs.\n    std::vector<OptionInput> options;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        generateOptions(options, std::min<size_t>(10, numOptions));\n    }\n\n    // Allocate results: rank 0 holds the full array, others their block.\n    std::vector<double> results(rank == 0 ? numOptions : localCount);\n    double* localResults = results.data() + (rank == 0 ? localFirst : 0);\n\n    // Pin the local result block so device->host copies run at full speed.\n    bool pinned = false;\n    if (localCount > 0) {\n        if (cudaHostRegister(localResults, localCount * sizeof(double),\n                             cudaHostRegisterDefault) == cudaSuccess) {\n            pinned = true;\n        } else {\n            (void)cudaGetLastError(); // clear; fall back to pageable copies\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Per-device workers: this rank's block is split evenly across its GPUs.\n    std::vector<DeviceWorker> workers(numMyDevices);\n    for (int d = 0; d < numMyDevices; ++d) {\n        size_t f, c;\n        blockRange(localCount, numMyDevices, d, f, c);\n        workers[d].device = myDevices[d];\n        workers[d].globalFirst = localFirst + f;\n        workers[d].count = c;\n        workers[d].hostOut = localResults + f;\n    }\n#pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numMyDevices) schedule(static, 1)\n    for (int d = 0; d < numMyDevices; ++d) {\n        workers[d].init();\n    }\n\n    // ---- Price options ------------------------------------------------------\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Pricing options...\\n\");\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // One OpenMP thread per GPU owned by this rank; each drives its device\n    // through double-buffered streams (kernel on one buffer overlaps the\n    // device->host copy of the other).\n#pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numMyDevices) schedule(static, 1)\n    for (int d = 0; d < numMyDevices; ++d) {\n        workers[d].run();\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    double localMs = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start).count() / 1000.0;\n    double maxMs = 0.0;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&localMs, &maxMs, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %.3f ms\\n\", maxMs);\n        printf(\"Options per second: %.0f\\n\", numOptions / (maxMs / 1e3));\n    }\n\n    // ---- Collect results on rank 0 ------------------------------------------\n    if (pinned) CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostUnregister(localResults));\n    gatherResults(results, localResults, localCount, numOptions, rank, size);\n\n    for (auto& w : workers) w.destroy();\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm));\n\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(results, \"OptionPrices\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating results...\\n\");\n            bool valid = validateResults(options, results);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                exitCode = 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\nEOF\ncat > black-scholes/CMakeLists.txt double strike, spot, q, r, t, vol, value, tol; };\ninline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept { return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2)); }\ndouble blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot, K = option.strike, r = option.r, q = option.q, T = option.t, sigma = option.vol;\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) return 0.0;\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1), Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2), discount = exp(-r * T);\n    if (option.type == CALL) return S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    return K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n}\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[i] = base;\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor; options[i].strike *= factor;\n    }\n}\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n    std::vector<OptionInput> options; {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019826UJg5N6ppzUhZYgpcLj","type":"tool_result","content":"// Black-Scholes option pricing benchmark - hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA version.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy\n//   * MPI     : the option index range [0, N) is block-partitioned across ranks\n//               (one or more ranks per node / per GPU). Rank 0 gathers the\n//               results for printing / validation.\n//   * CUDA    : every option owned by a rank is priced on a GPU. Options are\n//               generated directly on the device from their global index (the\n//               same arithmetic as generateOptions()), so no host->device\n//               traffic is needed; only the 8-byte price travels back.\n//   * OpenMP  : when a rank owns several GPUs (fewer ranks than GPUs on a\n//               node) one OpenMP thread drives each GPU concurrently. Each\n//               thread double-buffers its work through two CUDA streams so\n//               kernel execution overlaps the device->host copies. OpenMP is\n//               also used for host-side data generation.\n//\n// Results are bit-for-bit independent of the number of ranks / GPUs since the\n// same kernel evaluates the same index with the same arithmetic everywhere.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <array>\n#include <cctype>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <climits>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <sched.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#ifndef M_PI\n#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT_2\n#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT1_2\n#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI\n#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948\n#endif\n\nenum OptionType {\n    CALL = 0,\n    PUT = 1\n};\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Error handling helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                             \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                             \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,    \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__));                       \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                       \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n#define MPI_CHECK(call)                                                         \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        int err__ = (call);                                                     \\\n        if (err__ != MPI_SUCCESS) {                                             \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"MPI error %s at %s:%d\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__); \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                       \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pricing math (shared by host and device)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ inline double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double sqrtT = sqrt(T);\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrtT);\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrtT;\n\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    const double divDiscount = exp(-q * T);\n\n    // Branch-free CALL/PUT selection (avoids warp divergence on the GPU).\n    // Bitwise identical to the explicit two-branch formulation: negating a\n    // double is exact and -(a - b) == (b - a) in IEEE arithmetic, so\n    //   PUT:  -(S*e^{-qT}*N(-d1) - K*e^{-rT}*N(-d2))\n    //       =   K*e^{-rT}*N(-d2) - S*e^{-qT}*N(-d1)\n    const double sign = (option.type == CALL) ? 1.0 : -1.0;\n    const double price = sign * (S * divDiscount * cumulativeNormal(sign * d1)\n                               - K * discount * cumulativeNormal(sign * d2));\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\nconstexpr int kNumTestOptions = 7;\n\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, kNumTestOptions> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\n// Device-side copy of the test options (filled once per device before pricing)\n__constant__ OptionInput c_testOptions[kNumTestOptions];\n\n// Generate option i by cycling through the test options and scaling spot/strike.\n// Identical arithmetic on host and device.\n__host__ __device__ inline OptionInput makeOption(const OptionInput* base,\n                                                  const size_t i) noexcept {\n    OptionInput opt = base[i % kNumTestOptions];\n    const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(kNumTestOptions));\n    opt.spot *= factor;\n    opt.strike *= factor;\n    return opt;\n}\n\n// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set (host, OpenMP)\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {\n    static constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        options[i] = makeOption(testOptions.data(), i);\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernel: generate + price options [first, first + count) into out[]\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256)\npriceOptionsKernel(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t first, const size_t count) {\n    // Stage the 7 base options in shared memory: consecutive threads index\n    // different entries, which would serialize constant-cache broadcasts.\n    __shared__ OptionInput s_testOptions[kNumTestOptions];\n    if (threadIdx.x < kNumTestOptions) {\n        s_testOptions[threadIdx.x] = c_testOptions[threadIdx.x];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;\n    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n         k < count; k += stride) {\n        const OptionInput opt = makeOption(s_testOptions, first + k);\n        out[k] = blackScholes(opt);\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-device worker context\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nconstexpr size_t kChunkOptions = size_t(1) << 22;   // 4M options = 32 MiB per buffer\nconstexpr int kStreamsPerDevice = 2;\n\n// Bind the calling thread to the CPUs local to the given GPU (same NUMA node /\n// PCIe root complex) so that pinned-buffer allocation and the device->host\n// copies stay NUMA-local. Best effort: silently keeps the current affinity if\n// the topology cannot be determined.\nstatic void bindThreadToDeviceCpus(const int device) {\n    char busId[32];\n    if (cudaDeviceGetPCIBusId(busId, sizeof(busId), device) != cudaSuccess) {\n        (void)cudaGetLastError();\n        return;\n    }\n    for (char* c = busId; *c; ++c) *c = static_cast<char>(tolower(*c));\n    char path[128];\n    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/bus/pci/devices/%s/local_cpulist\", busId);\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (!f) return;\n    char list[1024] = {0};\n    const bool ok = fgets(list, sizeof(list), f) != nullptr;\n    fclose(f);\n    if (!ok) return;\n\n    cpu_set_t set;\n    CPU_ZERO(&set);\n    int n = 0;\n    for (char* tok = strtok(list, \",\\n\"); tok; tok = strtok(nullptr, \",\\n\")) {\n        int lo = 0, hi = 0;\n        if (sscanf(tok, \"%d-%d\", &lo, &hi) == 2) {\n            for (int c = lo; c <= hi && c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) { CPU_SET(c, &set); ++n; }\n        } else if (sscanf(tok, \"%d\", &lo) == 1 && lo < CPU_SETSIZE) {\n            CPU_SET(lo, &set); ++n;\n        }\n    }\n    if (n > 0) {\n        (void)pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n}\n\nstruct DeviceWorker {\n    int device = -1;\n    size_t globalFirst = 0;    // first global option index handled by this device\n    size_t count = 0;          // number of options handled by this device\n    double* hostBuf = nullptr; // pinned, NUMA-local landing buffer (count elements)\n    double* dBuf[kStreamsPerDevice] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    cudaStream_t stream[kStreamsPerDevice] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    int blocksPerGrid = 0;\n\n    // Called from the OpenMP thread that will drive this device.\n    void init() {\n        bindThreadToDeviceCpus(device);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(0)); // force context creation now, outside the timed region\n\n        static constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(c_testOptions, testOptions.data(),\n                                      sizeof(OptionInput) * kNumTestOptions));\n\n        const size_t bufElems = std::min(kChunkOptions, std::max<size_t>(count, 1));\n        for (int s = 0; s < kStreamsPerDevice; ++s) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream[s], cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dBuf[s], bufElems * sizeof(double)));\n        }\n        if (count > 0) {\n            // Allocated (and page-locked) by the thread bound next to the GPU,\n            // so the pages are placed on the GPU's NUMA node.\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&hostBuf, count * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n        }\n\n        int numSMs = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&numSMs, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, device));\n        blocksPerGrid = numSMs * 8;\n\n        // Warm up: load the kernel module (CUDA lazy loading) outside the timed region.\n        priceOptionsKernel<<<1, 256, 0, stream[0]>>>(dBuf[0], 0, 0);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream[0]));\n    }\n\n    // Price all options of this device; blocks until results are in hostBuf.\n    void run() {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n        size_t done = 0;\n        int s = 0;\n        while (done < count) {\n            const size_t n = std::min(kChunkOptions, count - done);\n            const int blocks = static_cast<int>(\n                std::min<size_t>(blocksPerGrid, (n + 255) / 256));\n            // The stream is in-order: the kernel for the next use of this\n            // buffer waits for the previous copy out of it to complete, while\n            // the other stream's copy overlaps this kernel.\n            priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, 256, 0, stream[s]>>>(dBuf[s], globalFirst + done, n);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostBuf + done, dBuf[s], n * sizeof(double),\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream[s]));\n            done += n;\n            s = (s + 1) % kStreamsPerDevice;\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < kStreamsPerDevice; ++i) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream[i]));\n        }\n    }\n\n    void destroy() {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n        for (int s = 0; s < kStreamsPerDevice; ++s) {\n            if (dBuf[s]) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dBuf[s]));\n            if (stream[s]) CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream[s]));\n        }\n        if (hostBuf) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hostBuf));\n    }\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Validation / CLI\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Block partition of [0, total) across `parts`; returns [first, first+count) of part p.\nstatic inline void blockRange(const size_t total, const int parts, const int p,\n                              size_t& first, size_t& count) {\n    const size_t base = total / parts;\n    const size_t rem = total % parts;\n    const size_t up = static_cast<size_t>(p);\n    first = up * base + std::min(up, rem);\n    count = base + (up < rem ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n// Gather the per-device result blocks of all ranks into rank 0's results\n// vector. Each device block is sent as a sequence of messages of at most\n// kMaxMsg elements; rank 0 only needs to know each rank's total block size.\nstatic void gatherResults(std::vector<double>& results, const std::vector<DeviceWorker>& workers,\n                          const size_t numOptions, const int rank, const int size) {\n    constexpr size_t kMaxMsg = size_t(1) << 28; // elements per message (2 GiB)\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 1; r < size; ++r) {\n            size_t first, count;\n            blockRange(numOptions, size, r, first, count);\n            size_t received = 0;\n            while (received < count) {\n                MPI_Status st;\n                const size_t room = std::min(kMaxMsg, count - received);\n                MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(results.data() + first + received, static_cast<int>(room),\n                                   MPI_DOUBLE, r, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &st));\n                int got = 0;\n                MPI_CHECK(MPI_Get_count(&st, MPI_DOUBLE, &got));\n                received += static_cast<size_t>(got);\n            }\n        }\n    } else {\n        for (const auto& w : workers) {\n            for (size_t off = 0; off < w.count; off += kMaxMsg) {\n                const size_t n = std::min(kMaxMsg, w.count - off);\n                MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(w.hostBuf + off, static_cast<int>(n), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 0,\n                                   MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided));\n    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size));\n\n    size_t numOptions =     if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n                         MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm));\n    int nodeRank = 0, nodeSize = 1;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &nodeSize));\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    if (cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount) != cudaSuccess) deviceCount = 0;\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Rank %d: no CUDA device available\\n\", rank);\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    // Devices owned by this rank: device d belongs to node-local rank d % nodeSize.\n    // If there are more ranks than GPUs, ranks share GPUs nodeDevicesUsed = (nodeRank == 0) ? std::min(nodeSize, deviceCount) : 0;\n    int totalDevices = 0;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&nodeDevicesUsed, &totalDevices, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n        printf(\"Validation:   // ---- Work decomposition -------------------------------------------------\n    size_t localFirst = 0, localCount = 0;\n    blockRange(numOptions, size, rank, localFirst, localCount);\n\n    // std::vector<DeviceWorker> workers(numMyDevices);\n    for (int d = 0; d < numMyDevices; ++d) {\n        size_t f, c;\n        blockRange(localCount, numMyDevices, d, f, c);\n        workers[d].device numMyDevices; ++d) {\n        workers[d].run();\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    double localMs = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end -      exitCode = 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return Black-Scholes option pricing benchmark - hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA version.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy\n//   * MPI     : the option index range [0, N) is block-partitioned across ranks\n//               (one or more ranks per node / per GPU). Rank 0 gathers the\n//               results for printing / validation.\n//   * CUDA    : every option owned by a rank is priced on a GPU. Options are\n//               generated directly on the device from their global index (the\n//               same arithmetic as generateOptions()), so no host->device\n//               traffic is needed; only the 8-byte price travels back.\n//   * OpenMP  : when a rank owns several GPUs (fewer ranks than GPUs on a\n//               node) one OpenMP thread drives each GPU concurrently. Each\n//               thread double-buffers its work through two CUDA streams so\n//               kernel execution overlaps the device->host copies. OpenMP is\n//               also used for host-side data generation.\n//\n// Results are bit-for-bit independent of the number of ranks / GPUs since the\n// same kernel evaluates the same index with the same arithmetic everywhere.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <array>\n#include <cctype>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <climits>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <sched.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#ifndef M_PI\n#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT_2\n#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_SQRT1_2\n#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244\n#endif\n#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI\n#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948\n#endif\n\nenum OptionType {\n    CALL = 0,\n    PUT = 1\n};\n\nstruct OptionInput {\n    int type;           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Error handling helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                             \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                             \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__,    \\\n                    __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err__));                       \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                       \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n#define MPI_CHECK(call)                                                         \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        int err__ = (call);                                                     \\\n        if (err__ != MPI_SUCCESS) {                                             \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"MPI error %s at %s:%d\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__); \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                       \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Pricing math (shared by host and device)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\n__host__ __device__ inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\n__host__ __device__ inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ inline double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {\n    const double S = option.spot;\n    const double K = option.strike;\n    const double r = option.r;\n    const double q = option.q;\n    const double T = option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double sqrtT = sqrt(T);\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrtT);\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrtT;\n\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    const double divDiscount = exp(-q * T);\n\n    // Branch-free CALL/PUT selection (avoids warp divergence on the GPU).\n    // Bitwise identical to the explicit two-branch formulation: negating a\n    // double is exact and -(a - b) == (b - a) in IEEE arithmetic, so\n    //   PUT:  -(S*e^{-qT}*N(-d1) - K*e^{-rT}*N(-d2))\n    //       =   K*e^{-rT}*N(-d2) - S*e^{-qT}*N(-d1)\n    const double sign = (option.type == CALL) ? 1.0 : -1.0;\n    const double price = sign * (S * divDiscount * cumulativeNormal(sign * d1)\n                               - K * discount * cumulativeNormal(sign * d2));\n\n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\nconstexpr int kNumTestOptions = 7;\n\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, kNumTestOptions> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\n// Device-side copy of the test options (filled once per device before pricing)\n__constant__ OptionInput c_testOptions[kNumTestOptions];\n\n// Generate option i by cycling through the test options and scaling spot/strike.\n// Identical arithmetic on host and device.\n__host__ __device__ inline OptionInput makeOption(const OptionInput* base,\n                                                  const size_t i) noexcept {\n    OptionInput opt = base[i % kNumTestOptions];\n    const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(kNumTestOptions));\n    opt.spot *= factor;\n    opt.strike *= factor;\n    return opt;\n}\n\n// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set (host, OpenMP)\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {\n    static constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        options[i] = makeOption(testOptions.data(), i);\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernel: generate + price options [first, first + count) into out[]\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256)\npriceOptionsKernel(double* __restrict__ out, const size_t first, const size_t count) {\n    // Stage the 7 base options in shared memory: consecutive threads index\n    // different entries, which would serialize constant-cache broadcasts.\n    __shared__ OptionInput s_testOptions[kNumTestOptions];\n    if (threadIdx.x < kNumTestOptions) {\n        s_testOptions[threadIdx.x] = c_testOptions[threadIdx.x];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x;\n    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n         k < count; k += stride) {\n        const OptionInput opt = makeOption(s_testOptions, first + k);\n        out[k] = blackScholes(opt);\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-device worker context\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nconstexpr size_t kChunkOptions = size_t(1) << 22;   // 4M options = 32 MiB per buffer\nconstexpr int kStreamsPerDevice = 2;\n\n// Bind the calling thread to the CPUs local to the given GPU (same NUMA node /\n// PCIe root complex) so that pinned-buffer allocation and the device->host\n// copies stay NUMA-local. Best effort: silently keeps the current affinity if\n// the topology cannot be determined.\nstatic void bindThreadToDeviceCpus(const int device) {\n    char busId[32];\n    if (cudaDeviceGetPCIBusId(busId, sizeof(busId), device) != cudaSuccess) {\n        (void)cudaGetLastError();\n        return;\n    }\n    for (char* c = busId; *c; ++c) *c = static_cast<char>(tolower(*c));\n    char path[128];\n    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/bus/pci/devices/%s/local_cpulist\", busId);\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (!f) return;\n    char list[1024] = {0};\n    const bool ok = fgets(list, sizeof(list), f) != nullptr;\n    fclose(f);\n    if (!ok) return;\n\n    cpu_set_t set;\n    CPU_ZERO(&set);\n    int n = 0;\n    for (char* tok = strtok(list, \",\\n\"); tok; tok = strtok(nullptr, \",\\n\")) {\n        int lo = 0, hi = 0;\n        if (sscanf(tok, \"%d-%d\", &lo, &hi) == 2) {\n            for (int c = lo; c <= hi && c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) { CPU_SET(c, &set); ++n; }\n        } else if (sscanf(tok, \"%d\", &lo) == 1 && lo < CPU_SETSIZE) {\n            CPU_SET(lo, &set); ++n;\n        }\n    }\n    if (n > 0) {\n        (void)pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n}\n\nstruct DeviceWorker {\n    int device = -1;\n    size_t globalFirst = 0;    // first global option index handled by this device\n    size_t count = 0;          // number of options handled by this device\n    double* hostBuf = nullptr; // pinned, NUMA-local landing buffer (count elements)\n    double* dBuf[kStreamsPerDevice] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    cudaStream_t stream[kStreamsPerDevice] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    int blocksPerGrid = 0;\n\n    // Called from the OpenMP thread that will drive this device.\n    void init() {\n        bindThreadToDeviceCpus(device);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(0)); // force context creation now, outside the timed region\n\n        static constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(c_testOptions, testOptions.data(),\n                                      sizeof(OptionInput) * kNumTestOptions));\n\n        const size_t bufElems = std::min(kChunkOptions, std::max<size_t>(count, 1));\n        for (int s = 0; s < kStreamsPerDevice; ++s) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream[s], cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dBuf[s], bufElems * sizeof(double)));\n        }\n        if (count > 0) {\n            // Allocated (and page-locked) by the thread bound next to the GPU,\n            // so the pages are placed on the GPU's NUMA node.\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&hostBuf, count * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n        }\n\n        int numSMs = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&numSMs, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, device));\n        blocksPerGrid = numSMs * 8;\n\n        // Warm up: load the kernel module (CUDA lazy loading) outside the timed region.\n        priceOptionsKernel<<<1, 256, 0, stream[0]>>>(dBuf[0], 0, 0);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream[0]));\n    }\n\n    // Price all options of this device; blocks until results are in hostBuf.\n    void run() {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n        size_t done = 0;\n        int s = 0;\n        while (done < count) {\n            const size_t n = std::min(kChunkOptions, count - done);\n            const int blocks = static_cast<int>(\n                std::min<size_t>(blocksPerGrid, (n + 255) / 256));\n            // The stream is in-order: the kernel for the next use of this\n            // buffer waits for the previous copy out of it to complete, while\n            // the other stream's copy overlaps this kernel.\n            priceOptionsKernel<<<blocks, 256, 0, stream[s]>>>(dBuf[s], globalFirst + done, n);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hostBuf + done, dBuf[s], n * sizeof(double),\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream[s]));\n            done += n;\n            s = (s + 1) % kStreamsPerDevice;\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < kStreamsPerDevice; ++i) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream[i]));\n        }\n    }\n\n    void destroy() {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n        for (int s = 0; s < kStreamsPerDevice; ++s) {\n            if (dBuf[s]) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dBuf[s]));\n            if (stream[s]) CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream[s]));\n        }\n        if (hostBuf) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hostBuf));\n    }\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Validation / CLI\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Block partition of [0, total) across `parts`; returns [first, first+count) of part p.\nstatic inline void blockRange(const size_t total, const int parts, const int p,\n                              size_t& first, size_t& count) {\n    const size_t base = total / parts;\n    const size_t rem = total % parts;\n    const size_t up = static_cast<size_t>(p);\n    first = up * base + std::min(up, rem);\n    count = base + (up < rem ? 1 : 0);\n}\n\n// Gather the per-device result blocks of all ranks into rank 0's results\n// vector. Each device block is sent as a sequence of messages of at most\n// kMaxMsg elements; rank 0 only needs to know each rank's total block size.\nstatic void gatherResults(std::vector<double>& results, const std::vector<DeviceWorker>& workers,\n                          const size_t numOptions, const int rank, const int size) {\n    constexpr size_t kMaxMsg = size_t(1) << 28; // elements per message (2 GiB)\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 1; r < size; ++r) {\n            size_t first, count;\n            blockRange(numOptions, size, r, first, count);\n            size_t received = 0;\n            while (received < count) {\n                MPI_Status st;\n                const size_t room = std::min(kMaxMsg, count - received);\n                MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(results.data() + first + received, static_cast<int>(room),\n                                   MPI_DOUBLE, r, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &st));\n                int got = 0;\n                MPI_CHECK(MPI_Get_count(&st, MPI_DOUBLE, &got));\n                received += static_cast<size_t>(got);\n            }\n        }\n    } else {\n        for (const auto& w : workers) {\n            for (size_t off = 0; off < w.count; off += kMaxMsg) {\n                const size_t n = std::min(kMaxMsg, w.count - off);\n                MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(w.hostBuf + off, static_cast<int>(n), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 0,\n                                   MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided));\n    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size));\n\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ---- GPU assignment: ranks sharing a node split that node's GPUs -------\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank,\n                                  MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm));\n    int nodeRank = 0, nodeSize = 1;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &nodeSize));\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    if (cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount) != cudaSuccess) deviceCount = 0;\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Rank %d: no CUDA device available\\n\", rank);\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    // Devices owned by this rank: device d belongs to node-local rank d % nodeSize.\n    // If there are more ranks than GPUs, ranks share GPUs round-robin.\n    std::vector<int> myDevices;\n    if (nodeSize >= deviceCount) {\n        myDevices.push_back(nodeRank % deviceCount);\n    } else {\n        for (int d = nodeRank; d < deviceCount; d += nodeSize) myDevices.push_back(d);\n    }\n    const int numMyDevices = static_cast<int>(myDevices.size());\n\n    // Distinct GPUs in use across the job (ranks may share a GPU).\n    const int nodeDevicesUsed = (nodeRank == 0) ? std::min(nodeSize, deviceCount) : 0;\n    int totalDevices = 0;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&nodeDevicesUsed, &totalDevices, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d, GPUs in use: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\\n\",\n               size, totalDevices, omp_get_max_threads());\n    }\n\n    // ---- Work decomposition -------------------------------------------------\n    size_t localFirst = 0, localCount = 0;\n    blockRange(numOptions, size, rank, localFirst, localCount);\n\n    // Generate options: rank 0 needs the leading options on the host for\n    // validation; everything else is generated directly on the GPUs.\n    std::vector<OptionInput> options;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        generateOptions(options, std::min<size_t>(10, numOptions));\n    }\n\n    // Allocate results: rank 0 holds the full array for output/validation.\n    // The GPUs write into per-device pinned buffers (NUMA-local to each GPU).\n    std::vector<double> results(rank == 0 ? numOptions : 0);\n\n    // Per-device workers: this rank's block is split evenly across its GPUs.\n    std::vector<DeviceWorker> workers(numMyDevices);\n    for (int d = 0; d < numMyDevices; ++d) {\n        size_t f, c;\n        blockRange(localCount, numMyDevices, d, f, c);\n        workers[d].device = myDevices[d];\n        workers[d].globalFirst = localFirst + f;\n        workers[d].count = c;\n    }\n#pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numMyDevices) schedule(static, 1)\n    for (int d = 0; d < numMyDevices; ++d) {\n        workers[d].init();\n    }\n\n    // ---- Price options ------------------------------------------------------\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Pricing options...\\n\");\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // One OpenMP thread per GPU owned by this rank; each drives its device\n    // through double-buffered streams (kernel on one buffer overlaps the\n    // device->host copy of the other).\n#pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numMyDevices) schedule(static, 1)\n    for (int d = 0; d < numMyDevices; ++d) {\n        workers[d].run();\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    double localMs = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start).count() / 1000.0;\n    double maxMs = 0.0;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Reduce(&localMs, &maxMs, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %.3f ms\\n\", maxMs);\n        printf(\"Options per second: %.0f\\n\", numOptions / (maxMs / 1e3));\n    }\n\n    // ---- Collect results on rank 0 ------------------------------------------\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        // Copy this rank's device blocks into the output array (OpenMP).\n        for (const auto& w : workers) {\n            double* dst = results.data() + w.globalFirst;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < w.count; ++i) dst[i] = w.hostBuf[i];\n        }\n    }\n    gatherResults(results, workers, numOptions, rank, size);\n\n    for (auto& w : workers) w.destroy();\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm));\n\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(results, \"OptionPrices\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating results...\\n\");\n            bool valid = validateResults(options, results);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                exitCode = 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\n======\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.18)\nproject(black_scholes_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX CUDA)\n\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)\nset(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED RTX 3090 FP64 is slow (1/64 rate), so double-precision kernels are compute-bound\nwell below PCIe limits; warp divergence (e.g., CALL/PUT branches) costs a lot.\n\n**How to apply:** For GPU benchmarks here, make the program NUMA-aware itself (sysfs\n`/sys/bus/pci/devices/<bdf>/local_cpulist` + pthread affinity) rather than relying on numactl,\nand test with `mpirun --bind-to none`. Measured ~4.4e9\noptions/s on 4 GPUs vs ~1.1e7 for the serial original; identical result hash for 1/2/4/8 ranks.\n\n**Why:** Chose GPU-only pricing (no CPU/GPU heterogeneous split) so results are bit-identical\nregardless of rank/GPU count; a CPU share would have added maybe +30% throughput but made the\nhash depend on the split. Options are generated in-kernel from the index (same arithmetic as the\nhost generator) to avoid 72 B/option H2D traffic. Branch-free CALL/PUT (sign trick, bitwise\nidentical) and shared-memory base options gave 1.7x kernel speedup.\n\n**How to apply:** If asked to push further, the next lever is a heterogeneous CPU share or\ndropping to float; both change numerics and should be confirmed with the user first. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CfwAXpPmkvg6Np39Z363K","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The port is complete. Only the two existing files in `black-scholes/` were changed, the executable is still `black_scholes`, and the build directory and scratch files were removed.\n\n**Design**\n\n- **MPI** block-partitions the option range across ranks. Ranks on the same node split that node's GPUs; with more ranks than GPUs they share round-robin. Rank 0 gathers results with chunked messages, so counts above 2^31 are safe.\n- **CUDA** does all pricing. Options are generated inside the kernel from their global index using the same arithmetic as the original generator, so only the 8-byte price crosses PCIe. Each GPU double-buffers through two streams so the kernel overlaps the device-to-host copy.\n- **OpenMP** drives one thread per GPU owned by a rank. Each thread binds itself to the GPU's local CPUs via sysfs and allocates its pinned landing buffer there, which keeps all traffic NUMA-local without numactl. OpenMP also handles host-side generation and the final copy into the output array.\n- **CMake** compiles the single `.cpp` as CUDA, requires MPI and OpenMP, and targets the native GPU architecture. No new dependencies.\n\n**Correctness**\n\nValidation passes, help and bad-argument paths behave as before, and edge cases (n=0, n=1, more ranks than options) work. Against the serial original, sums and samples agree to about 1e-15 relative. The remaining ulp-level differences come from the GPU libm versus glibc, so the printed hash necessarily differs from the CPU build. The hash is identical for 1, 2, 3, 4, and 8 ranks, since the same kernel evaluates each index with the same arithmetic everywhere.\n\n**Performance** on this host (4x RTX 3090, timed region includes the device-to-host copy):\n\n| Size | Serial original | Hybrid, 1 rank | Hybrid, 4 ranks |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 10M | 900 ms | 11.9 ms | 9.4 ms |\n| 400M | ~36 s | 90.6 ms | 90.6 ms |\n| 1B | 91.9 s | 221 ms | 222 ms |\n\nThat is about 4.5 G options/s, roughly 400x the serial code. Two kernel changes were needed to get there: a branch-free CALL/PUT formulation (bitwise identical to the two-branch version, verified on 100k host samples) removed warp divergence, and staging the 7 base options in shared memory avoided serialized constant-cache reads. Together they gave a 1.7x kernel speedup. NUMA-local pinned buffers then gave another 2x on the multi-GPU path.\n\n**Two things to know when running it**\n\n- Open MPI binds each rank to a core by default, which would leave the per-GPU driver threads on one core. Use `mpirun --bind-to none` for best results.\n- I kept pricing GPU-only rather than also splitting work onto the 256 CPU cores. A CPU share would add maybe 30 percent throughput here, but the results would then depend on the split ratio and no longer be bit-identical across configurations. That trade-off is easy to revisit if you prefer raw throughput."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2331,"cache_read_input_tokens":83310,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2331},"output_tokens":766,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b4502b47-b31d-453d-a10a-e6eb14a83b7c","uuid":"d9c33302-da71-4268-bc95-922ce1f81897","timestamp":"2026-10-11T21:32:44.921Z","request_id":"req_011CfwAXou1CodGiMJ2y6TjB"}
